                                RD-5725-1.1                                               I
TRUEVIEW5725                                                                      Registers Definition

                                               Register Map


          Segment
                            0              1               2              3               4              5
        Address
          00~0F          Status
                                         Input                                        Memory          ADC
                        Register                                                     Chapter 05     Chapter 11
                                       Formatter
          10~1F       (Read Only)                    De-interlace
                                      Chapter 01
          20~2F        Chapter 00                     Chapter 02                     Capture &
                                                                        Video
                                                                      Processor      Playback,
          30~3F                                                                      Chapter 06
                                      HD-bypass                      Chapter 08                      Sync Proc
          40~4F                                                                      Read FIFO,
                     Miscellaneous                                                   Write FIFO     Chapter 12
                                      Chapter 03
                     Chapter 04                                                      Chapter 07
          50~5F

          60~6F
                                     Mode Detect
                                                                        PIP
          70~7F
                                                                     Chapter 08
                                      Chapter 10
          80~8F
                         OSD
          90~9F
                       Chapter 09
          A0~AF

          B0~BF

          C0~CF

          D0~DF

          E0~EF



Note:
         1. Address marked with         is not existed in 5725.
         2. All registers (except chapter 01 status register is read only) have default value “0x00” after power up.
         3. All registers require segment for access. Segment is defined in address F0.
            For example:
              S1_46 means F0 must be set to 1 before accessing 46
              S1_46=8D equal following operation:
                                  F0 = 01
                                  46 = 8D

Chapter 00．                  STATUS REGISTERS


INPUT MODE STATUS 00                                                                            REG S0_00, RO

             7           6       5            4             3              2              1            0
 Bit                                          IF_STATUS_[7:0]


       Bit       Name                Function
                                     Vertical stable indicator
       0         IF_STATUS_[0]
                                     When =1, means input vertical timing is stable
                                     Horizontal stable indicator
       1         IF_STATUS_[1]
                                     When =1, means input horizontal timing is table
                                     H & V stable indicator
       2         IF_STATUS_[2]
                                     When =1, means input H/V timing are both stable
                                     NTSC interlace indicator
       3         IF_STATUS_[3]
                                     When =1, means input is NTSC interlace (480i) source
                                     NTSC progressive indicator
       4         IF_STATUS_[4]
                                     When =1, means input is NTSC progressive (480P) source
                                     PAL interlace indicator
       5         IF_STATUS_[5]
                                     When =1, means input is PAL interlace (576i) source
                                     PAL progressive indicator
       6         IF_STATUS_[6]
                                     When =1, means input is PAL progressive (576P) source
                                     SD mode indicator
       7         IF_STATUS_[7]
                                     When =1, means input is SD mode (480i, 480P, 576i, 576P)




                                             RD-5725-1.1                                                   00—1
TRUEVIEW5725                                                             Registers Definition


INPUT MODE STATUS 01                                                                          REG S0_01, RO

             7           6        5            4             3              2             1              0
 Bit                                          IF_STATUS_[15:8]


       Bit       Name                 Function
                                      VGA 60Hz mode
       0         IF_STATUS_[8]
                                      When =1, means input is VGA (640x480) 60Hz mode
                                      VGA 75Hz mode
       1         IF_STATUS_[9]
                                      When =1, means input is VGA (640x480) 75Hz mode
                                      VGA 85 Hz mode
       2         IF_STATUS_[10]
                                      When =1, means input is VGA (640x480) 85Hz mode
                                      VGA mode indicator
       3         IF_STATUS_[11]
                                      When =1, means input is VGA (640x480) source, include 60Hz/75Hz/85Hz
                                      SVGA 60Hz mode
       4         IF_STATUS_[12]
                                      When =1, means input is SVGA (800x600) 60Hz mode
                                      SVGA 75Hz mode
       5         IF_STATUS_[13]
                                      When =1, means input is SVGA (800x600) 75Hz mode
                                      SVGA 85Hz mode
       6         IF_STATUS_[14]
                                      When =1, means input is SVGA (800x600) 85Hz mode
                                      SVGA mode indicator
       7         IF_STATUS_[15]
                                      When =1, means input is SVGA (800x600) source, include 60Hz/75Hz/85Hz




INPUT MODE STATUS 02                                                                          REG S0_02, RO

             7           6        5            4             3              2             1              0
 Bit                                          IF_STATUS_[23:16]


       Bit       Name                 Function
                                      XGA 60Hz mode
       0         IF_STATUS_[16]
                                      When =1, means input is XGA (1024x768) 60Hz mode
                                      XGA 70Hz mode
       1         IF_STATUS_[17]
                                      When =1, means input is XGA (1024x768) 70Hz mode
                                      XGA 75Hz mode
       2         IF_STATUS_[18]
                                      When =1, means input is XGA (1024x768) 75Hz mode
                                      XGA 85Hz mode
       3         IF_STATUS_[19]
                                      When =1, means input is XGA (1024x768) 85Hz mode
                                      XGA mode indicator
       4         IF_STATUS_[20]
                                      When =1, means input is XGA (1024x768) source, include 60/70/75/85Hz
                                      SXGA 60Hz mode
       5         IF_STATUS_[21]
                                      When =1, means input is SXGA (1280x1024) 60Hz mode
                                      SXGA 75Hz mode
       6         IF_STATUS_[22]
                                      When =1, means input is SXGA (1280x1024) 75Hz mode

Chapter 01．                   INPUT FORMATTER REGISTERS


INPUT_FORMATTER 00                                                                                            REG S1_00, R/W

            7          6            5              4                3                2                    1             0
                  IF_PRGRSV_C                                                IF_UV_REVER IF_MATRIX_BYP IF_IN_DRE
 Bit IF_HS_FLIP       NTRL
                                 IF_VS_SEL   IF_SEL16BIT IF_SEL_656
                                                                                  T            S        G_BYPS


       Bit      Name                     Function
                                         Input pipe by pass
                                         Use the falling or rising edge of clock to get the input data.
        0       IF_IN_DREG_BYPS
                                         0: Clock input data on the falling edge of ICLK.
                                         1: Clock input date on the rising edge of ICLK.
                                         Rgb2yuv matrix bypass
                                         If source is yuv24bit, bypass the rgb2yuv matrix.
        1       IF_MATRIX_BYPS           0:source is 24bit RGB. Do rgb2yuv.
                                         1: data bypass.
                                         8bit to 16bit convert Y/UV flip control
                                         If input is 8bit data, when it convert to 16bit, this bit control Y and UV order:
        2       IF_UV_REVERT
                                         0: Keep the designed order
                                         1: Flip the Y and UV order
                                         Select CCIR656 data
                                         If input data is 8bit CCIR656 mode, choose the 656 data path.
        3       IF_SEL_656               0: input is CCIR 601 mode. Choose the CCIR601mode timing.
                                         1: input is CCIR 656 mode. Choose the CCIR656 mode timing.
                                         Select 16bit data
                                         If source data is 16bit. Choose the 16bits data path.
                                         Use in conjunction with register sel_24bit to choose the input data format.
        4                                                                    Sel_16bit                  Sel_24bit
                IF_SEL16BIT
                                           8bit 656/601 input                     0                          0
                                           16bit 601 input                        1                          0
                                           24bit yuv/rgb 601 input                *                          1

                                         Vertical sync select
                                         Choose the periodical or virtual vertical timing.
        5       IF_VS_SEL                0: choose the VCR mode timing generation.
                                         1: choose the normal mode timing generation.
                                         Select progressive data
                                         Progressive mode. Choose the progressive data.
        6       IF_PRGRSV_CNTRL          0: source is interlaced.
                                         1: source is progressive.
                                         Horizontal sync flip control
                                         Control the horizontal sync output from CCIR process
        7       IF_HS_FLIP               0: keep the original horizontal sync.
                                         1: flip horizontal sync.




                                                  RD-5725-1.1                                                                01—1
TRUEVIEW5725                                                                          Registers Definition

INPUT_FORMATTER 01                                                                                             REG S1_01, R/W

              7          6                5             4               3                2                 1               0
       IF_SEL24BI                               IF_TAP6_BYP
 Bit       T
                             IF_Y_DELAY
                                                     S
                                                            IF_V_DELAY            IF_U_DELAY         IF_UV_FLIP      IF_VS_FLIP


         Bit      Name                        Function
                                              Vertical sync flip control
                                              Control the vertical sync output from CCIR process
          0       IF_VS_FLIP
                                              0: keep original vertical sync.
                                              1: flip vertical sync.
                                              YUV 422to444 UV flip control
                                              Control the U and V order in yuv422to444 conversion.
          1       IF_UV_FLIP                  0: keep original U and V order.
                                              1: exchange the U and V order.
                                              U data select in YUV 422to444 conversion
                                              Select original U data or 1-clock delayed U data, so that it can align with V data.
          2       IF_U_DELAY
                                              0: select original U data after dmux.
                                              1: select 1-clock delayed U data after dmux.
                                              V data select in YUV 422to444 conversion
                                              Select original V data or 1-clock delayed V data, so that it can align with U data.
          3       IF_V_DELAY                  0: select original V data after dmux.
                                              1: select 1-clock delayed V data after dmux.
                                              Tap6 interpolator bypass control in YUV 422to444 conversion
          4                                   Select the data if pass the tap6 interpolator or not.
                  IF_TAP6_BYPS
                                              0: the data will pass the tap6 interpolator.
                                              1: the data will not pass the tap6 interpolator
                                              Y data pipes control in YUV422to444 conversion
                                              Control the Y data pipe delay, so that it can align with U and V.
                                                IF_Y_DELAY           Y data delay pipes
         6-5      IF_Y_DELAY                         00                      1
                                                     01                      2
                                                     10                      3
                                                     11                      4
                                              Select 24bit data
          7       IF_SEL24BIT                 If input source is 24bit data, choose the 24bit data path.




                                                       RD-5725-1.1                                                             01—2
TRUEVIEW5725                                                                           Registers Definition

INPUT_FORMATTER 02                                                                                             REG S1_02, R/W

               7          6             5             4                3                 2                 1           0

 Bit    GN2UNSIGN

                                                                                     IF_HS_SEL_LPF
                                                                                                       N


         Bit       Name                     Function
                                            Select the write enable for line double
                                            If the input is HD source, this bit will be set to 1.
         0         IF_SEL_WEN
                                            0: if the source is SD data.
                                            1: if the source is HD data.
                                            Low pass filter or interpolator selection
                                            The low pass filter and interpolator data path is combined together.
          1        IF_HS_SEL_LPF            0: select interpolator data path.
                                            1: select low pass filter data path.
                                            Combined INT and LPF data path bypass control
                                            If the data can’t do horizontal scaling-down, bypass the INT/LPF data path.
         2         IF_HS_INT_LPF_BYPS
                                            0: select the INT/LPF data path.
                                            1: bypass the INT/LPF data path
                                            Phase adjustment bypass control
                                            If the data can’t do phase adjustment, this bit should be set to 1.
         3         IF_HS_PSHIFT_BYPS        0: select phase adjustment data path.
                                            1: bypass phase adjustment.
                                            Tap11 LPF bypass control in YUV444to422 conversion
                                            Select the data if pass the tap11 LPF or not.
         4         IF_HS_TAP11_BYPS
                                            0: the data will pass the tap11 low pass filter.
                                            1: the data will not pass the tap11 low pass filter
                                            Y data pipes control in YUV444to422 conversion
                                            Control the Y data pipe delay, so that it can align with UV.
                                              IF_HS_Y_DELAY             Y data delay pipes
        6-5        IF_HS_Y_PDELAY                   00                          1
                                                    01                          2
                                                    10                          3
                                                    11                          4
                                            UV data select
                                            If UV is signed, select the unsigned UV data
         7         IF_HS_UV_SIGN2UNSIGN
                                            0: select the original UV
                                            1: select the UV after sign processing

Chapter 03．                HD_BYPS REGISTERS


HD_BYPS 00                                                                                                REG S1_30, R/W

           7          6              5             4               3                 2                    1       0
                                                            HD_SEL_BLK             HD_MATRIX_B HD_IN_DRE
 Bit                      RESERVED
                                                                _IN
                                                                       HD_DYN_BYPS
                                                                                       YPS      G_BYPS


       Bit     Name                      Function
                                         Input retiming bypass
                                         Use the falling or rising edge of clock to get the input data.
       0       HD_IN_DREG_BYPS
                                         0: Clock input data on the falling edge
                                         1: Clock input date on the rising edge
                                         YUV2RGB conversion bypass control
                                         Available only when input source is YUV source
       1       HD_MATRIX_BYPS            0: YUV2RGB convert
                                         1: bypass YUV2RGB function
                                         Dynamic range bypass control
                                         If the input is YUV data, it must do dynamic range.
       2       HD_DYN_BYPS               0: input is YUV data, do dynamic range .
                                         1: input is RGB data, bypass dynamic range
                                         Blank select
                                         Choose the input blank or generated blank use sync.
       3       HD_SEL_BLK_IN             0: choose the blank that sync generated.
                                         1: choose the input blank, if the input is DVI data.

       7-4     RESERVED

Chapter 04．                       MISCELLANEOUS REGISTERS


PLL648         CONTROL 00                                                                         REG S0_40, R/W

               7              6        5             4             3              2           1            0
 Bit   RESERVED                      PLL_MS                    PLL_ADS         PLL_IS     PLL_DIVBY2Z   PLL_CKIS


         Bit       Name                    Function
                                           CKIS, PLL source clock selection
         0         PLL_CKIS                When = 0, PLL use OSC clock
                                           When = 1, PLL use input clock
                                           DIVBY2Z, PLL source clock divide bypass
         1         PLL_DIVBY2Z             When = 0, PLL source clock divide by two
                                           When = 1, PLL source clock bypass divide
                                           IS, ICLK source selection
         2         PLL_IS                  When = 0, ICLK use PLL clock
                                           When = 1, ICLK use input clock
                                           ADS, input clock selection
         3         PLL_ADS                 When = 0, input clock is from PCLKIN(pin40)
                                           When = 1, input clock is from ADC
                                           MS[2:0], memory clock control
                                           When = 000, memory clock =108MHz
                                           When = 001, memory clock = 81MHz
                                           When = 010, memory clock from FBCLK (pin110)
        6-4        PLL_MS                  When = 011, memory clock = 162MHz
                                           When = 100, memory clock = 144MHz
                                           When = 101, memory clock = 185MHz
                                           When = 110, memory clock = 216MHz
                                           When = 111, memory clock = 129.6Mhz
                                           Reserved
         7         RESERVED




                                                   RD-5725-1.1                                                 04—1
TRUEVIEW5725                                                                     Registers Definition

Chapter 05．                  MEMORY REGISTERS


MEMORY CONTROLLER 00                                                                                         REG S4_00, R/W

             7           6            5              4               3                2                  1            0
 Bit                                                  MEM_INI_REG


       Bit       Name                     Function
                                          SDRAM Idle Period Control and IDLE Done Select: (default 0)
                                                      MEM_INI_REG [1:0]                   #of VS (vertical syn)
                                                    0                       0                        2
       1-0       MEM_INI_REG [1:0]
                                                    0                       1                        3
                                                    1                       0                        1
                                                    1                       1                 R_MSOFTH
                                          Software Control SDRAM Idle Period:
       2         MEM_INI_REG [2]          When this bit is 1, software programming will control the idle period to access
                                          memory.this bit is useful only when the register r_mslidl[1:0] sets 2’b11.
                                          Reserved
       3         MEM_INI_REG [3]

                                          SDRAM Reset Signal:
       4         MEM_INI_REG [4]          When this bit is 1, will generate 5-mmclk pulse, and reset memory controller
                                          timing, data pipe and state machine;
                                          Reserved
       5         MEM_INI_REG [5]

                                          Initial Cycle Mode Select:
       6         MEM_INI_REG [6]          When this bit is 1, then during initial period, the mode cycle will go before
                                          refresh cycle; otherwise refresh cycle will be before mode cycle.
                                          SDRAM Start Initial Cycle:
                                          This register should work with the register 80/[2:0];
       7         MEM_INI_REG [7]          When this bit is 1, memory controller initial cycle enable;
                                          When this bit is 0, memory controller initial cycle disable.

             7           6            5        4                     3                2                  1            0

                                        Capture request exchange with PlayBack low request and Read FIFO low
                                        request
       1         MEM_REQ_PB_RFF_CAP
                                        When this bit is 0: play back low req > read FIFO low req > capture req
                                        When this bit is 1: cap req > play back low req > read FIFO low req
                                        Write FIFO request priority exchange with capture request
       2         MEM_REQ_WFF_CAP        When this bit is 1, capture request >write FIFO request,
                                        When this bit is 0, write FIFO request > capture request
                                        RESERVED
       7-3       RESERVED

                                    Capture Noise Reduction Frame Status Offset
                                    For NTSC and PAL, Noise Reduction will save 4 or 6 frame data, for Play back

CAPTURE 01                                                                                           REG S4_21, R/W

            7           6           5                  4              3                2         1               0
     CAP_ADR_AD CAP_VRST_F CAP_SAFE_GU          CAP_DOUBL CAP_BUF_ST CAP_FF_HAL CAPTURE_EN
 Bit     D_2     FRST_EN     ARD_EN
                                       RESERVED
                                                 E_BUFFER    A_INV     F_REQ       ABLE


      Bit       Name                    Function
                                        Enable capture
                                        When it’s set 1, capture will be turn on.
       0        CAPTURE_ENABLE
                                        When it’s set 0, capture will be turn off.

                                        Request generated when capture FIFO half
       1        CAP_FF_HALF_REQ         When set to 1, request generated when capture FIFO half.
                                        When set to 0, request generated when capture FIFO write pointer is 1.
                                        Capture double buffer status invert before output
       2        CAP_BUF_STA_INV         When set to 1, double buffer status invert.
                                        When set to 0, double buffer status doesn’t change.
                                        Enable double buffer
       3        CAP_DOUBLE_BUFFER       When set to 1, enable double buffer.
                                        When set to 0, disable double buffer.
                                        RESERVED
       4        RESERVED

                                        Enable safe guard function
       5        CAP_SAFE_GUARD_EN       When set to 1, turn on safe guard function.
                                        When set to 0, turn off safe guard function.
                                        Enable input v-sync reset FIFO
       6        CAP_VRST_FFRST_EN       When set to 1, enable feed back v-sync reset FIFO.
                                        When set to 0, disable feed back v-sync reset FIFO.
                                        Enable address add by 2
       7        CAP_ADR_ADD_2           When set to 1,address added by 2 per pixel,
                                        When set to 0,added by 1 per pixel.

PLAY BACK 00                                                                                                   REG S4_2B, R/W

              7               6            5              4               3               2               1            0
                 PB_2FRAME_ PB_DB_BUFF PB_DB_FIELD                                                                 PB_CUT_REF
 Bit   PB_ENABLE
                   EXCHG      ER_EN        _EN

                                                                                                                      RESH


        Bit           Name                     Function
                                               Disable refresh request generation
         0            PB_CUT_REFRESH           When set to 1, disable refresh request generation.
                                               When set to 0, enable refresh request generation.
                                               Enable playback request mode
                                                PB_REQ_SEL                   PBHREQ                     PBLREQ
                                                00                           0                          Low request
        2-1           PB_REQ_SEL
                                                01                           0                          High request
                                                10                           Low request                0
                                                11                           High request               Low request
                                               Enable VDS input to select playback output or de-interlace data out
         3            PB_BYPASS                When this bit is 1, select de-interlace data out to VDS.
                                               When this bit is 0, select playback output to VDS.
                                               Enable double field display
         4            PB_DB_FIELD_EN           When set to 1, enable double field display.
                                               When set to 0, disable double field display.
                                               Enable double buffer
         5            PB_DB_BUFFER_EN          When set to 1, enable double buffer.
                                               When set to 0, disable double buffer.
                                               Exchange playback two frames output data
         6            PB_2FRAME_EXCHG          When set to 1, exchange playback current frame with past frame and output.
                                               When set to 0, don’t exchange.

                                               Enable Playback
         7            PB_ENABLE
                                               When it’s set 1, play back will be on work, or will not work.

CAPTURE AND PLAYBACK SHARED 00                                                                           REG S4_30, R/W

             7          6              5             4               3               2               1              0
 Bit                        RESERVED                                              PB_CAP_NOISE_CMD


       Bit       Name                      Function
                                           Capture Noise Reduction Command
                                           0: disable noise reduce function
                                           1: turn on PAL mode 2 (50hz to 50hz) and storage in memory 5 frames
                                           2: turn on PAL mode 3
                                           5: turn on NTSC mode 2 and storage memory 3 frames
       3-0       PB_CAP_NOISE_CMD          6: turn on NTSC mode 3
                                           9: turn on PAL mode 2 (50hz to 50hz, 50hz to 60hz, 50hz to 100hz) and storage
                                           memory 6 frames.
                                           D: turn on NTSC mode 2 (60hz to 60hz, 60hz to 120hz) and storage memory 4
                                           frames
                                           Note: in 50 to 100hz and 60 to 120,we must turn on [4] = 1 In playback
                                           RESERVED
       7-4       RESERVED

WRITE FIFO 01                                                                                            REG S4_42, R/W

             7          6            5              4               3                2               1                0
     WFF_FF_STA WFF_REQ_OVWFF_ADR_AD WFF_VRST_F WFF_SAFE_G WFF_FF_STA WFF_FF_HAL
 Bit  TUS_SEL       ER        D_2      F_RST       UARD       _INV      F_REQ
                                                                                 WFF_ENABLE


       Bit       Name                    Function
                                         Enable write FIFO
       0         WFF_ENABLE              When it’s set 1, write FIFO will be turn on.
                                         When it’s set 0, write FIFO will be turn off.
                                         Request generated when FIFO half
       1         WFF_FF_HALF_REQ         When set to 1, request generated when FIFO half.
                                         When set to 0, request generate when FIFO write pointer is 1.
                                         Write FIFO status invert
       2         WFF_FF_STA_INV          When set to 1, write FIFO status invert.
                                         When set to 0, write FIFO status don’t change.
                                         Enable write FIFO safe guard
       3         WFF_SAFE_GUARD          When set to 1, enable write FIFO safe guard.
                                         When set to 0, disable write FIFO safe guard.
                                         Enable input V-sync reset FIFO
       4         WFF_VRST_FF_RST         When set to 1, enable feedback v-sync reset FIFO.
                                         When set to 0, disable feedback v-sync reset FIFO.
                                         WRITE FIFO Address count select:
       5         WFF_ADR_ADD_2           When it’s set to 1, address added by 2 per pixel.
                                         When it’s set to 0, address added by 1 per pixel.
                                         WRITE FIFO Horizontal Request End
       6         WFF_REQ_OVER            When this bit set 1, the final write FIFO request of one line is in the horizontal
                                         blank rising edge, set 0 write FIFO request will free run
                                         WRITE FIFO HALF STATUS SELECT
       7         WFF_FF_STATUS_SEL       When set to 1, request generated when FIFO is half.
                                         When set to 0, request generated when c FIFO is delm’s value.

VDS_PROC 00                                                                                          REG S3_00, R/W

            7          6          5              4               3               2               1               0
                   VDS_HALF_ VDS_VSCALE_ VDS_HSCALE_ VDS_FIELD_ VDS_DFIELD_ VDS_FIELDA VDS_SYNC_E
 Bit VDS_SRESET       EN        BYPS        BYPS        FLIP        EN         B_EN         N


      Bit       Name                  Function
                                      External sync enable, active high
                                      This bit enable sync lock mode.
                                        vds_flock_en (1A[4])            vds_sync_en                     VDS timing
       0        VDS_SYNC_EN
                                                 0                           0                           Free run
                                                 0                           1                           Sync lock
                                                 1                           X                          Frame lock
                                      ABAB double field mode enable
       1        VDS_FIELDAB_EN        In field double mode, when this bit is 1, VDS works in ABAB mode, otherwise it
                                      works in AABB mode.
                                      Double field mode enable active high
       2        VDS_DFIELD_EN         This bit enable field double mode, ex, frame rate from 50Hz to 100Hz, or from
                                      60Hz to 120Hz. When this bit is 1, the output timing is interlaced.
                                      Flip field control.
       3        VDS_FIELD_FLIP        This bit is field flip control bit, it only used in interlace mode. When it is 1, it
                                      inverts the output field.
                                      Horizontal scale up bypass control, active high
       4        VDS_HSCALE_BYPS       When this bit is 1, data will bypass horizontal scale up process.
                                      Vertical scale up bypass control, active high
       5        VDS_VSCALE_BYPS
                                      When this bit is 1, data will bypass vertical scale up process.
                                      Horizontal scale up bypass control, active high
       6        VDS_HALF_EN

                                      Horizontal scale up bypass control, active high
       7        VDS_SRESET            When this bit is 1, it reset the VDS_PROC internal module ds_video_enhance,

 Bit                                             VDS_FRAME_RST [7:0]


       Bit       Name                      Function
                                           Frame reset period control bit [7:0]
                                           This field indicates how many frames VSD_PROC locked at each time, it based
                                           on the input vertical sync.
       7-0       VDS_FRAME_RST [7:0]
                                           EX: FRAME_RST=4, this means VDS_PROC will lock every 5 frames, (This
                                           frame number is counts at every input vertical sync, the frame number of
                                           VDS_PROC output maybe different)

                                           Frame lock enable, active high

       Bit       Name                  Function
                                       422 to 444 conversion UV flip control
       0         VDS_UV_FLIP           This bit is used to flip UV, when this bit is 1, UV position will be flipped.
                                       UV 422 to 444 conversion U delay
       1         VDS_U_DELAY           When this bit is 1, U will delay 1 clock, otherwise, no delay for internal pipe.
                                       UV 422 to 444 conversion V delay
       2         VDS_V_DELAY           When this bit is 1, V will delay 1 clock, otherwise, no delay for internal pipe.
                                       Tap6 filter in 422 to 444 conversion bypass control, active high
       3         VDS_TAP6_BYPS         This bit is the UV interpolation filter enable control; when this bit is 1, UV bypass
                                       the filter
                                       Y compensation delay control bit [1:0] in 422 to 444 conversion
                                       To compensation the pipe of UV, program this field can delay Y from 1 to 4
                                       clocks.

                                             Noise reduction threshold value bit [3:0]
        3-0       VDS_NR_MI_THRESH           Noise-reduction threshold value. When MI is smaller than the threshold value,
                                             the noise reduction is enabled. Otherwise it is not.
                                             High noisy picture index enable, active high
         4        VDS_NR_EN_H_NOISY          Enable high noisy index from de-interlacer, it means the picture’s noise is very
                                             large.

         5        RESERVED

                                             Global still index enable, active high
         6        VDS_NR_EN_GLB_STILL        This bit enables the global still signal.
                                             Menu mode control for global still index (used for debug)
                                             This bit is the user defined menu mode for global still signal, when it is 1, the
                                             global still signal is 1, the following is the detail.

                                                                          PIP_V_DELAY PIP_U_DELAY PIP_UV_FLIP


         Bit       Name                  Function
                                         422 to 444 conversion UV flip control
         0         PIP_UV_FLIP
                                         This bit is used to flip UV, when this bit is 1, UV position will be flipped.
                                         UV 422 to 444 conversion U delay
         1         PIP_U_DELAY           When this bit is 1, U will delay 1 clock, otherwise, no delay for internal pipe.
                                         UV 422 to 444 conversion V delay
         2         PIP_V_DELAY           When this bit is 1, V will delay 1 clock, otherwise, no delay for internal pipe.
                                         Tap3 filter in 422 to 444 conversion bypass control, active high
         3         PIP_TAP3_BYPS         This bit is the UV interpolation filter enable control; when this bit is 1, UV bypass
                                         the filter

                                         To compensation the pipe of UV, program this field can delay Y from 1 to 4
                                         clocks.

                                         When it is 0, select 24-bit sub-picture.

PIP 01                                                                                                     REG S3_81, R/W

                                                                                           RESERVED
                                                                                                                    BYPS


         Bit       Name                    Function
                                           YUV to RGB color space conversion bypass control, active high
                                           When this bit is 1, YUV data will bypass the YUV to RGB conversion, the output
         0         PIP_CONVT_BYPS          will still be YUV data.
                                           When this bit is 0, YUV data will do YUV to RGB conversion, the output will be
                                           RGB data.

         2-1       RESERVED

                                           Input data bypass the negedge trigger control, active high
         3         PIP_DREG_BYPS           When this bit is 0, input data will triggered by falling edge clock,
                                           When this bit is 1, the input data will bypass this falling edge clock delay.

         7         PIP_EN                  When this bit is 1, PIP insertion is enabled, otherwise, no PIP
